import React, { useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"; import { Crown, Info } from "lucide-react"; // ---- shared design tokens ------------------------------------------- const INK = "#16231C"; const PAPER = "#F3EEDF"; const GOLD = "#C69A3B"; const PARTY_COLORS = { anc: "#006600", da: "#005BA6", mk: "#FDB913", eff: "#852A2A", ifp: "#FF0000", pa: "#388F35", vf: "#EC8713", action: "#12796F", acdp: "#2E5E4E", udm: "#8B6F9E", rise: "#5B7DA6", bosa: "#B57A4A", atm: "#748C54", aljama: "#9C5B45", ncc: "#4A6FA5", pac: "#A23E2E", uat: "#6E8272", good: "#C99A3C", }; const PARTY_ORDER = ["anc", "da", "mk", "eff", "ifp", "pa", "vf", "action"]; // minor parties that each cleared roughly 0.2%+ of the 2024 national ballot, // listed with their real 2024 national vote share. No sliders for these — // whatever's left over after the sliders above is split between them in // proportion to those real 2024 shares (relative to each other). const MINOR_PARTIES = [ { key: "acdp", share2024: 0.60 }, { key: "udm", share2024: 0.49 }, { key: "rise", share2024: 0.42 }, { key: "bosa", share2024: 0.41 }, { key: "atm", share2024: 0.40 }, { key: "aljama", share2024: 0.24 }, { key: "ncc", share2024: 0.23 }, { key: "pac", share2024: 0.23 }, { key: "uat", share2024: 0.22 }, { key: "good", share2024: 0.18 }, ]; const MINOR_TOTAL_2024 = MINOR_PARTIES.reduce((s, m) => s + m.share2024, 0); const MINOR_KEYS = MINOR_PARTIES.map((m) => m.key); const MINOR_WEIGHT = {}; MINOR_PARTIES.forEach((m) => (MINOR_WEIGHT[m.key] = m.share2024 / MINOR_TOTAL_2024)); // national 2024 baseline (National ballot, IEC final results) — also used // as the default input, and as the swing reference point for the map. // PA, VF+, and ActionSA are included as they each cleared 1% nationally. const NATIONAL_BASELINE = { anc: 40.2, da: 21.8, mk: 14.6, eff: 9.5, ifp: 3.9, pa: 2.1, vf: 1.4, action: 1.2 }; function othersOf(input) { const sum = PARTY_ORDER.reduce((s, p) => s + (input[p] || 0), 0); return Math.max(0, 100 - sum); } // split a remainder pool across the minor parties using their relative 2024 weight function splitMinors(pool) { const out = {}; MINOR_PARTIES.forEach((m) => (out[m.key] = pool * MINOR_WEIGHT[m.key])); return out; } // A province/municipality's local share normally moves in lockstep with the // national swing (base + (input - nationalBaseline)). But if a party has a // much higher local baseline than its national one, that additive model can // leave a positive local share even after the national slider hits 0 — the // party never actually disappears locally. Below 20% of a party's 2024 // national result, taper its local share linearly down to a true 0 at 0%, // instead of just continuing the additive swing. function taperedLocalShare(base, input, nationalBase) { base = base || 0; if (nationalBase <= 0) return Math.max(0, base + input); const ratio = input / nationalBase; if (ratio >= 0.2) return Math.max(0, base + (input - nationalBase)); const valueAt20 = Math.max(0, base - 0.8 * nationalBase); return Math.max(0, valueAt20 * (ratio / 0.2)); } // ======================================================================= // Seat allocation (largest remainder / Hare quota) — feeds the hemicycle // ======================================================================= function allocateSeats(shares, totalSeats) { const total = Object.values(shares).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1; let allocated = 0; const withQuota = Object.entries(shares).map(([key, votes]) => { const exact = (votes / total) * totalSeats; const whole = Math.floor(exact); allocated += whole; return { key, votes, quotaSeats: whole, remainder: exact - whole }; }); let remaining = totalSeats - allocated; const byRemainder = [...withQuota].sort((a, b) => b.remainder - a.remainder); const bonusKeys = new Set(); for (let i = 0; i < remaining && i < byRemainder.length; i++) bonusKeys.add(byRemainder[i].key); const out = {}; withQuota.forEach((p) => { out[p.key] = p.quotaSeats + (bonusKeys.has(p.key) ? 1 : 0); }); return out; } // ---- hemicycle seat layout ------------------------------------------- function buildHemicycle(totalSeats) { if (totalSeats <= 0) return []; const rows = Math.max(4, Math.min(9, Math.round(Math.sqrt(totalSeats / 2.4)))); const innerR = 115; const outerR = 272; const step = rows > 1 ? (outerR - innerR) / (rows - 1) : 0; const radii = Array.from({ length: rows }, (_, i) => innerR + i * step); const radiusSum = radii.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); let counts = radii.map((r) => Math.round((r / radiusSum) * totalSeats)); let diff = totalSeats - counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); let i = counts.length - 1; while (diff !== 0 && counts.length > 0) { if (diff > 0) { counts[i]++; diff--; } else if (counts[i] > 0) { counts[i]--; diff++; } i = i - 1 < 0 ? counts.length - 1 : i - 1; } const seats = []; radii.forEach((r, rowIdx) => { const n = counts[rowIdx]; if (n <= 0) return; for (let s = 0; s < n; s++) { const angle = n === 1 ? Math.PI / 2 : Math.PI - (s * Math.PI) / (n - 1); seats.push({ x: 300 + r * Math.cos(angle), y: 292 - r * Math.sin(angle), angle, r }); } }); seats.sort((a, b) => b.angle - a.angle || a.r - b.r); return seats; } // ======================================================================= // Provincial swing map // ======================================================================= // Minor-party province baselines, sourced from full provincial-ballot result // tables (Wikipedia per-province election articles) where available. A 0 // means that party did not clear a meaningful share there in 2024 — most // of these parties only won seats in one or two provinces. const PROVINCE_BASELINE = { // exact 2024 National Ballot Cape"}}]}; function buildProjector(geojson, targetW, targetH, padding) { let minLon = Infinity, maxLon = -Infinity, minLat = Infinity, maxLat = -Infinity; const visit = (coords) => { if (typeof coords[0] === "number") { const [lon, lat] = coords; if (lon < minLon) minLon = lon; if (lon > maxLon) maxLon = lon; if (lat < minLat) minLat = lat; if (lat > maxLat) maxLat = lat; } else { coords.forEach(visit); } }; geojson.features.forEach((f) => visit(f.geometry.coordinates)); const midLat = (minLat + maxLat) / 2; const latCorrection = Math.cos((midLat * Math.PI) / 180); const spanX = (maxLon - minLon) * latCorrection; const spanY = maxLat - minLat; const availW = targetW - padding * 2; const availH = targetH - padding * 2; const scale = Math.min(availW / spanX, availH / spanY); const offsetX = padding + (availW - spanX * scale) / 2; const offsetY = padding + (availH - spanY * scale) / 2; return (lon, lat) => [(lon - minLon) * latCorrection * scale + offsetX, (maxLat - lat) * scale + offsetY]; } function ringToPath(ring, project) { return ( ring .map(([lon, lat], i) => { const [x, y] = project(lon, lat); return `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${x.toFixed(2)},${y.toFixed(2)}`; }) .join(" ") + "Z" ); } function featureToPath(feature, project) { const { type, coordinates } = feature.geometry; if (type === "Polygon") return coordinates.map((ring) => ringToPath(ring, project)).join(" "); if (type === "MultiPolygon") return coordinates.map((polygon) => polygon.map((ring) => ringToPath(ring, project)).join(" ")).join(" "); return ""; } // ======================================================================= // Page // ======================================================================= export default function ElectionExplorer() { const [input, setInput] = useState({ ...NATIONAL_BASELINE }); const cap = Math.max(0, 100 - othersSum); const v = Math.min(cap, Math.max(0, Number(value))); return { ...prev, [party]: v }; }); }; const inputOthers = othersOf(input); const nationalOthers = othersOf(NATIONAL_BASELINE); // ---- seat allocation, shared shares object (8 tracked + 10 minor parties) ---- const shares = useMemo(() => { const s = {}; PARTY_ORDER.forEach((p) => (s[p] = input[p] || 0)); Object.assign(s, splitMinors(inputOthers)); return s; }, [input, inputOthers]); const seatCounts = useMemo(() => allocateSeats(shares, totalSeats), [shares]); const seatCoords = useMemo(() => buildHemicycle(totalSeats), []); const majority = Math.floor(totalSeats / 2) + 1; const coalitionHasMajority = coalitionSeats >= majority; // ---- provincial swing map, driven by the same input ---- const provinceProjections = useMemo(() => { const out = {}; Object.entries(PROVINCE_BASELINE).forEach(([prov, base]) => { const raw = {}; }); const total = Object.values(raw).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1; const norm = {}; Object.entries(raw).forEach(([p, v]) => (norm[p] = (v / total) * 100)); let winner = PARTY_ORDER[0]; let winnerPct = -1;
